### [1] [Mass as pairing: an explicit Majorana mass on half of a generation does not seesaw in symmetric mass generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07735)
*Piotr S. Topa*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个四维对称质量生成（SMG）模型，其中一半Spin(10)-类代粒子被赋予显式Majorana质量源，研究另一半在无凝聚相中的行为；发现无源部分的所有单费米子质量项在源作用的对称群下平均为零，故轻质量项与轻-重混合需依赖自发对称破缺；数值模拟（至8⁴格点）表明：在对称能隙相中源被强烈屏蔽，无源部分单费米子响应极小且两体关联增强但未出现轻质量通道；临界点处显式质量导致σ道坍缩，轻组分趋近自由场行为而非幂律标度；未观测到一阶相变、Z₄奇响应由格点复合算符携带、正定性类具有味民主特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解对称质量生成（SMG）机制下显式质量源如何影响未受源作用的费米子自由度，特别是轻质量项与轻-重混合是否必然要求自发对称破缺，并检验其在格点规范理论中的实现方式与相结构。

Method: 基于四维格点QFT构造Spin(10)-类生成的半代SMG模型，引入色民主的Δ_R型显式Majorana质量源作用于所有味的右手中微子二重态；通过蒙特卡洛模拟（体积达8⁴）测量单费米子读出、对关联函数、σ道传播子、临界耦合漂移等可观测量，并严格依据预注册测试协议进行分析。

Result: （i）对称能隙相中显式源被屏蔽至自由值的≤1.1%，无源半代单费米子响应稳定在自由值0.3%，其对关联从0.2%升至1.2–1.7%（h=2），但无任何轻质量通道超越自由传播子增长；（ii）临界点处显式质量使σ道崩溃，轻组分呈现饱和型交叉趋近自由行为，非幂律；未定位临界耦合y_c(h)，未测量p→0时轻传播子的极点/零点；无L≤8下的一阶相变信号；Z₄-奇响应由格点复合算符承载；正定性类具味民主性。

Conclusion: 在SMG框架下，无源费米子的轻质量项及轻-重混合必须由自发对称破缺产生——该结论在格点上严格成立且具普适性；数值结果支持该命题，同时揭示了显式质量源诱导的非平凡临界行为与对称性响应结构。

Abstract: A fermion mass pairs a field with a partner of conjugate charge: elementary (Higgs-Dirac), self-conjugate (seesaw) or a three-fermion composite (symmetric mass generation, SMG). We give half of a Spin(10)-like generation an explicit Majorana mass in a four-dimensional SMG model and ask what the other half does in a phase with no condensate. With the source on, every mass-type term of the unsourced half averages to zero under the symmetry group of the sourced action, so a light mass term, and a seesaw's light-heavy mixing, requires a spontaneously broken symmetry; exact on the lattice and a general proposition. The source is colour-democratic (a Delta_R-type mass on every flavour's right-handed taste doublet); the single-nu_R split lies outside the positivity class used but inside the symmetry statement. Measured up to 8^4: (i) in the symmetric-gap phase, with the source screened to at most 1.1% of free, the unsourced half's single-fermion readout stays at 0.3% of free while its pair susceptibility rises from 0.2% to 1.2-1.7% at h=2; on like mode sets it falls with the volume, on (4^4,8^4) no inequivalent light mass channel grows faster than free, and symmetry breaking is not resolved at L<=8; (ii) at the critical point the explicit mass collapses the critical sigma channel and moves the light half toward free as a saturating crossover, not a power law; direction tests (MIXED by their pre-registered letter) are consistent with a critical coupling rising with the mass; y_c(h) is not located. Pole versus zero of the light propagator at p->0 is not measured. The outcomes are readings after pre-registered tests that mostly returned no verdict. Also: no first-order signature of the merged transition at L<=8, a Z_4-odd response handed to a lattice composite, and flavour democracy of the positivity class. Every claim has an evidence class and a runnable check.

</details>

### [2] [Inverse construction of exact atmospheric models and the 3.7--4.4\,mHz acoustic resonances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07073)
*O. V. Kaptsov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究大气中由大型火山喷发和地震激发的3.7与4.4 mHz大气振荡，通过线性声-重力波模型导出含势Klein–Gordon方程；构造了可显式积分的大气剖面，提出Darboux–Crum型‘透明’大气模型，并用Rosen–Morse势精确求解共振条件，成功解释观测中两主频模式的不同衰减机制（隧穿vs. 过阈辐射），且全波数值模拟复现了六次事件中的3.7–3.8 mHz基频振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释观测到的3.7和4.4 mHz大气-固体地球耦合振荡的频谱特征与差异性阻尼行为，尤其是为何基频（3.7 mHz）比泛音（4.4 mHz）衰减更弱。

Method: 基于垂直传播线性声-重力波在静力学理想气体大气中的传播时间公式，导出含势Q(τ)的Klein–Gordon方程；运用可积系统方法（零能解构造、Darboux–Crum变换、Rosen–Morse势解析解）构建物理自洽的大气剖面；结合全波数值模拟验证模型对真实事件的预测能力。

Result: 1) 给出所有允许正零能解的势Q(τ)对应的大气剖面显式构造法；2) 构造Darboux–Crum型平滑大气，精确匹配US Standard Atmosphere（11–100 km），且对高于截止频率的垂直波完全透明、无4.4 mHz阻尼泛音、基频模完全局域；3) Rosen–Morse势给出闭式超几何共振条件，预言3.76 mHz（Q=173）与4.37 mHz（Q=20）共振，分别对应低于/高于中间层截止势垒顶部的隧穿/辐射泄漏机制；4) 全波模拟在7个事件中成功复现6个的3.7–3.8 mHz基频。

Conclusion: 观测频谱要求中间层存在有限厚度的截止势垒连接低层大气与热层；3.7 mHz模式为隧穿主导的准束缚态，4.4 mHz模式为辐射主导的准连续态；该框架统一解释了大气振荡的频率、品质因子与激发选择性。

Abstract: Large explosive eruptions and earthquakes excite atmospheric oscillations near 3.7 and 4.4\,mHz that couple to the solid Earth. For vertically propagating linear acoustic--gravity waves in a hydrostatic ideal-gas atmosphere, the travel-time formulation gives a Klein--Gordon equation with a potential $Q(τ)$. We establish three main results. First, for every potential admitting a positive zero-energy solution, the corresponding atmospheric profiles are constructed explicitly by quadrature; this extends the known reflectionless profiles of constant $Q$. Second, Darboux--Crum potentials give a smooth atmosphere that reproduces the U.S. Standard Atmosphere between 11 and 100\,km to within 3\,K and is exactly transparent to vertical waves above its cut-off; it supports no damped overtone near 4.4\,mHz, and its ground-confined mode below the cut-off is perfectly trapped. The observed spectrum therefore requires a cut-off barrier of finite thickness connecting the lower atmosphere to the thermosphere. Third, a Rosen--Morse barrier gives an exactly solvable atmosphere with a closed-form hypergeometric resonance condition. Its resonances at 3.76 and 4.37\,mHz, with leakage quality factors 173 and 20, explain the different damping of the two oscillations: the fundamental lies below the top of the mesospheric cut-off barrier and leaks by tunnelling, whereas the overtone lies above it. Full-wave computations for event-specific atmospheres reproduce the reported 3.7--3.8\,mHz fundamental oscillation in all six events, of seven considered, in which such a mode was identified.

</details>


### [3] [Araki Cohomology of the de Sitter Scalar Discrete Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07090)
*Maryam Bajalan,Jean-Pierre Gazeau,Hamed Pejhan,Petr Siegl*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于dS协变的Krein实现，揭示了标量离散级列表示Π_{p,0}的Araki上同调结构，构造出Gupta-Bleuler四重态，实现了对物理表示与其复共轭的上同调区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解dS时空中量子场论的协变表示结构，特别是离散级列表示的非分裂不可分解性及其物理态甄别机制。

Method: 运用Krein空间框架与Gupta-Bleuler三重态构造，结合Araki上同调理论，分析SO₀(1,4)表示的扩展类与adjunction关系，并分解中间可约扇区。

Result: 确立Krein-Gupta-Bleuler构造是Araki标准三重态的具体实现；发现非平凡一阶上同调类编码不可分解结构；中间扇区分解导致四层结构，形成Gupta-Bleuler四重态。

Conclusion: 标量离散级列表示具有由上同调类刻画的精细结构，Gupta-Bleuler四重态提供了比三重态更优的物理态与复共轭态的共变区分。

Abstract: Building on the recently established de Sitter (dS)-covariant indecomposable Krein realization of the scalar discrete-series unitary irreducible representations $Π_{p,0}$, $p=1,2,\cdots$, of $\mathrm{SO}_0(1,4)$, naturally organized into a Gupta-Bleuler triplet, we uncover its underlying Araki-cohomological structure. We show that the Krein-Gupta-Bleuler construction provides a concrete realization of Araki's standard Gupta-Bleuler triplet, with its non-split indecomposable structure encoded by non-trivial first cohomology classes related by adjunction. We further show that the reducible intermediate sector naturally resolves into its two irreducible components, inducing a corresponding decomposition of the extension classes and yielding a four-layer refinement of Araki's standard triplet. The resulting Gupta-Bleuler quadruplet provides a finer cohomological resolution, distinguishing the physical representation from its complex-conjugate sector.

</details>


### [4] [Conformal invariance of the six-vertex model's height function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07454)
*Emile Averous,Hugo Duminil-Copin,Tiancheng He,Piet Lammers,Ioan Manolescu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在单连通区域上具有分段常数边界条件的六顶点模型的高度函数，在标度极限下收敛于带狄利克雷边界的高斯自由场（GFF），从而确立了该模型标度极限的共形不变性；该结果适用于一个连续谱参数区间Δ，是首个对连续族模型建立共形不变性的成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立六顶点模型标度极限的严格共形不变性，拓展此前仅限离散参数点（如冰点Δ=1/2）的结果，并统一处理多个相关统计力学模型。

Method: 结合全平面GFF收敛性、反射正定性（Reflection Positivity）及定性估计，通过边界条件适配与紧致性论证控制高度函数的宏观行为。

Result: 六顶点模型高度函数在适当归一化下弱收敛于带狄利克雷边界的GFF；该收敛对Δ∈(−1,−1/2)∪{0附近}成立，构成首个连续参数区间的共形不变性证明。

Conclusion: 六顶点模型在所涉Δ范围内具有共形不变的标度极限，其高度场普适类为Dirichlet GFF；该框架可推广至q=4的FK渗流与Ashkin-Teller模型。

Abstract: In this paper, we show that the height function of the six-vertex model in simply connected domains with piecewise constant boundary conditions converges to the Gaussian Free Field (GFF) with Dirichlet boundary conditions. This implies the conformal invariance of the scaling limit of the model.
  The argument relies on the full plane GFF convergence combined with Reflexion Positivity and qualitative estimates. The proof works for a wide range of spectral parameters $Δ$ (among which $Δ$ close to $0$, and between $-1$ and $-1/2$) making this paper the first instance of a conformal invariance result for a continuum of models. The paper has consequences for other models, including the FK percolation with cluster-weight $q=4$ and the Ashkin-Teller model, which we will pursue in future work.

</details>


### [5] [Spontaneous symmetry breaking at any temperature in a local one-dimensional model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07693)
*Andrew Lucas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个具螺旋对称性ℤ²⋊ℤ的一维经典统计模型，其局域态空间为ℤ²；证明在任意有限温度β下存在不可数无穷多DLR态，参数化于ℝ²，且表现出ℤ²味对称性的自发破缺；域壁结构解释了渐近对称扇区的分离；有限尺寸周期边界下的测度集中现象支持该破缺解释；推广至ℝ²态空间时，非紧致连续螺旋对称性亦被破缺，不违反Mermin-Wagner定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非紧致、非阿贝尔对称性（特别是螺旋对称ℤ²⋊ℤ）在低维系统中能否发生自发对称破缺，挑战传统Mermin-Wagner直觉，并厘清紧致性与平移不变性在对称性破缺中的作用。

Method: 基于DLR方程构造Gibbs态；利用螺旋对称性分解态空间；通过域壁配置实现ℝ²参数化族；结合有限体积周期边界下的测度集中分析（Laplace方法/大偏差估计）；对比紧致（ℤ²）与非紧致（ℝ²）对称性情形。

Result: ① 对ℤ²态空间模型：任意β∈(0,∞)存在ℝ²标度的不可数DLR态族，ℤ²味对称自发破缺；② DLR态具域壁结构，分隔两个渐近对称扇区；③ 有限体积周期系统中Gibbs态测度集中于互不相交、远距分离的ℤ²轨道簇；④ ℝ²态空间推广下，连续螺旋对称必破缺，因该对称群非紧致且不与平移对易。

Conclusion: 一维系统可发生自发对称破缺，只要对称群是非紧致或非阿贝尔且不与平移对易；Mermin-Wagner定理的紧致性前提至关重要；螺旋对称性提供了一类普适机制，使低维热涨落无法恢复非紧致对称性。

Abstract: We present a one-dimensional classical statistical physics model on an infinite lattice with nearest-neighbor interactions and local state space $\mathbb{Z}^2$, with a ``helical" symmetry group $\mathbb{Z}^2\rtimes \mathbb{Z}$ mixing flavor symmetry with spatial translation. For any $0<β<\infty$, we construct an uncountable infinity of DLR (Gibbs) states parameterized by $\mathbb{R}^2$, with a free $\mathbb{Z}^2$ flavor symmetry group action; as such, we interpret the model as exhibiting spontaneous symmetry breaking at any temperature. The most general DLR state we construct is interpreted as an infinite sequence of localized domain walls separating a pair of asymptotic ``symmetry sectors". Although any normalizable Gibbs state necessarily breaks a countably infinite freely-acting symmetry group, a natural regularization of the problem to (arbitrarily) large but finite systems with periodic boundary conditions has measure concentration in disjoint and far-separated symmetry-related clusters in the (now unique) Gibbs state, which justifies our interpretation of spontaneous symmetry breaking on the infinite line. A modification of the model with local state space $\mathbb{R}^2$ must break a continuous helical symmetry at any temperature. This is not a contradiction with the Mermin-Wagner Theorem, because this continuous helical symmetry is non-compact and does not commute with translation.

</details>


### [6] [A mathematical analogy between certain hollow vortex and bubble flows - existence, uniqueness, and identical boundary shapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07709)
*Valentin D. Kunz,Saleh Tanveer,Darren G. Crowdy*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究二维无粘流体中两类稳态自由边界问题：带剪切和应变的常涡度空心涡，以及带表面张力、线性应变和环量的无旋气泡；通过Crowdy-Tanveer建立的涡-泡对应关系，统一其数学框架，并分别给出局部存在性、唯一性与解析性结果，且发现一个标量可解性条件等价于共形映射恒等式，该条件保证两类问题具有相同自由边界形状。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示空心涡与恒压气泡这两类物理机制迥异的自由边界问题之间的深层数学联系，并拓展Crowdy-Tanveer提出的涡-泡对应理论至含剪切与高阶应变的更一般外流情形。

Method: 采用复势方法与共形映射理论，将两类问题统一为Riemann-Hilbert型边值问题；对涡问题延拓序列空间不动点方法（Crowdy-Tanveer），对气泡问题构造非线性奇异积分算子并导出标量可解性条件；结合实解析隐函数定理与复分析工具证明解的存在性、唯一性与边界解析性。

Result: 1) 空心涡问题在含简单剪切与三阶/线性应变外流下存在局部唯一实解析解；2) 气泡问题导出一标量实解析可解性曲面，在其上存在局部唯一实解析边界解，且速度场可全纯延拓；3) 该标量条件等价于一函数型共形映射恒等式；4) 当参数满足该条件时，两类问题自由边界几何重合。

Conclusion: 空心涡与恒压气泡虽物理机制不同（有旋vs无旋、无表面张力vs有表面张力），但在特定参数约束下共享同一自由边界形状，其本质源于复势结构的共形不变性与边值问题的数学同构性；该结果深化了自由边界流体力学中‘形式相似性蕴含几何一致性’的认识。

Abstract: We study two physically distinct steady free-boundary problems in a two-dimensional inviscid fluid: a hollow vortex in a constant-vorticity flow subject to simple shear, third order and linear strain at infinity, without surface tension, and an irrotational flow past a constant-pressure bubble subject to surface tension, linear strain, and circulation at infinity. Building on the mathematical vortex-bubble correspondence recently developed by Crowdy and Tanveer, we show that both problems are linked by a common mathematical formulation. For the vortex problem, we extend the sequence-space fixed-point approach of Crowdy and Tanveer to obtain new local existence, uniqueness, and analyticity results. For the bubble problem, we develop a separate existence theory, reducing it to a nonlinear singular integral operator fixed-point problem and deriving a scalar solvability condition that defines a realanalytic surface in the physical parameter space. On this surface we obtain locally unique bubble solutions with real-analytic boundaries and holomorphically extendible velocity fields. Finally, we prove that the scalar solvability condition is equivalent to a functional conformal mapping identity. The scalar condition between the parameters is enough for the vortex and bubble problems to have the same free-boundary shape, despite their different exterior flows.

</details>


### [7] [Pulsation of quantum walk on finite graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07768)
*Taisuke Hosaka,Etsuo Segawa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究加权图上的离散时间量子游走，其中割边被赋予小权重ε；当ε→0时，系统退化为若干连通分量；在时间尺度t=Θ(ε^{-1/2})下，跨分量的发现概率由约化图上的连续时间波动方程描述，该方程等价于经典弹簧-质点系统的牛顿运动方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子游走在具有弱连接结构的图上的宏观动力学行为，特别是如何从离散、微观的量子演化中涌现出连续、宏观的有效动力学。

Method: 采用多尺度渐近分析与图约化技术，将原图按割边分离为连通分量，构造约化图（顶点对应分量、边对应弱边），并推导ε→0极限下主导阶的连续时间波动方程。

Result: 在t=Θ(ε^{-1/2})时间尺度下，跨连通分量的发现概率渐近满足由对称加权拉普拉斯算子驱动的连续时间波动方程；该方程形式等同于约化图上经典弹簧-质点系统的牛顿方程；主导阶概率与各分量内部结构无关。

Conclusion: 离散时间量子游走在含弱边割集的图上，在适当慢时间尺度下，有效动力学由约化图上的波动方程完全刻画，揭示了量子游走与经典力学系统的深层对应关系。

Abstract: We study discrete-time quantum walks on weighted graphs, where every edge in an edge cut set is assigned a small weight $ε>0$. The parameter $ε$ represents the strength of the connections through the cut edges: as $ε\to 0$, these connections vanish, and the graph decomposes into the connected components obtained by removing the cut edges. We refer to these weighted cut edges as {\it weak edges}. We show that, for sufficiently small $ε$ and on the time scale $t=Θ(ε^{-1/2})$, the finding probabilities between the connected components are asymptotically described by a continuous-time wave equation on a reduced graph, whose vertices represent the connected components and whose edges represent the weak edges connecting them. The wave equation is governed by a symmetric weighted Laplacian determined by the reduced graph and the number of arcs contained in the components. Consequently, finding probabilities of leading-term are independent of the detailed internal structures of the components. We further show that this wave equation has the same form as Newton's equation of motion for a classical spring-mass system on the reduced graph.

</details>


### [8] [Convergence Without Finite Exactness in the Eigenstate Quantum Bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07775)

The Casimir energy density $ρ= -π^2/(720 r^4)$ ensures the Null Energy Condition is violated throughout the throat region, as demonstrated by the negative behavior of $(ρ+ p_r)$ and $(ρ- |p_r|)$ across all parameter values. Abstract: We study the pseudospectrum of the Kerr black hole for gravitational perturbations with spin weight $s=2$, the case of greatest interest in gravitational-wave astronomy. TL;DR: 本文通过Kiselev黑洞基准模型，研究了高插值精度与物理参数可识别性之间的区别；发现结合ringdown与光子几何信息可显著提升参数w_q的可识别性，并改善Jacobian条件数和最小奇异值，但逆估计器的鲁棒性仍依赖于具体模型架构和数据分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分插值精度与物理参数可识别性，检验在强引力场中多信使观测量（如ringdown与光子环）是否能打破参数简并，特别是Kiselev黑洞中电荷状态方程参数w_q与变形振幅k之间的退化。

Method: 采用受控的Kiselev黑洞时空作为基准，结合验证过的类时发射体模型与三维类光测地线射击计算；利用k=0边界（即Schwarzschild极限）作为解析已知的零控制；系统比较ringdown单独、ringdown+光子几何联合的信息内容；评估Jacobian条件数、最小奇异值、归一化MAE、split-conformal覆盖度及非学习型最近模型逆映射性能；辅以161相与321相审计分析前向特征敏感性与模型漂移。

Result: ringdown+光子几何相较ringdown单独：最小奇异值中位增益4.54×，Jacobian条件数改善2.28×；可识别w_q的归一化MAE从0.309降至0.087；非学习型逆映射证实增益不限于特定学习架构；161相审计显示典型预测偏移9.48×10⁻⁴；321相审计可解决剩余前向特征对比；但冻结HGB与随机森林尾部漂移超出鲁棒性阈值。

Conclusion: 物理可观测量（ringdown与光子几何）具有互补性，支持参数可识别性提升；但逆估计器的鲁棒性并非普适，而是依赖于模型选择、训练策略与分布一致性——本工作为理论可识别性设定了严格基准，而非观测约束。

Abstract: High interpolation accuracy does not establish that physical parameters are identifiable. We study this distinction in a controlled Kiselev black hole benchmark with a validated timelike-emitter and three-dimensional null-geodesic shooting calculation. The exact $k=0$ boundary provides an analytically known null control because the spacetime becomes Schwarzschild and independent of $w_q$. Ringdown is informative for the deformation amplitude $k$, while photon geometry supplies an independent response direction that resolves much of the $w_q$ degeneracy. Relative to ringdown alone, ringdown plus photon geometry gives a median pointwise $4.54\times$ gain in minimum singular value and a $2.28\times$ improvement in Jacobian condition number. In grouped physical interpolation, identifiable-$w_q$ normalized mean absolute error decreases from 0.309 to 0.087. Random splitting is more optimistic, while directional extrapolation is substantially harder and split-conformal coverage degrades under distribution shift. A non-learned nearest-model inverse shows that the gain is not confined to the tested learned architectures. A uniform 161-phase audit gives a typical normalized prediction shift of $9.48\times10^{-4}$, while a targeted 321-phase audit resolves the remaining forward-feature comparisons. However, frozen HGB and random-forest tail shifts exceed prespecified robustness thresholds. Thus, physical observable complementarity is supported, but inverse-estimator robustness remains conditional. This is a theoretical identifiability benchmark, not an observational constraint.

</details>

Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次观测到ψ(3686) → μ⁺μ⁻η′ 的电磁Dalitz衰变，统计显著性达6.1σ，测得分支比为(4.1±1.0±0.4)×10⁻⁷；其与ψ(3686)→γη′的分支比之比为(3.3±0.9)×10⁻³，与矢量介子主导（VMD）模型预言一致；μ/ e 衰变通道比为0.22±0.07，亦与VMD预期相符，未发现轻子味普适性破缺迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验轻子味普适性（LFU）及矢量介子主导（VMD）模型在类粲偶素电磁衰变中的适用性；探索ψ(3686)的稀有Dalitz衰变机制。

Method: 基于BESIII实验采集的(2712.4±14.3)×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，采用多变量分析（MVA）与不变质量拟合技术，结合蒙特卡洛模拟进行效率修正与背景抑制，提取信号强度并计算分支比及比值。

Result: 首次观测到ψ(3686)→μ⁺μ⁻η′，显著性6.1σ；分支比ℬ=(4.1±1.0_stat±0.4_syst)×10⁻⁷；ℬ(μ⁺μ⁻η′)/ℬ(γη′)=(3.3±0.9)×10⁻³；ℬ(μ⁺μ⁻η′)/ℬ(e⁺e⁻η′)=0.22±0.07。

Conclusion: 结果与矢量介子主导模型在1σ内一致，未发现轻子味普适性破缺，支持VMD对ψ(3686)→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻η′过程的有效描述。

Abstract: Utilizing $(2712.4 {\pm} 14.3)\times10^{6}~ψ(3686)$ events collected by the BESIII detector at the symmetric $e^+ e^-$ collider BEPCII, we report the first observation of the electromagnetic Dalitz decay $ψ(3686) \to μ^+ μ^-η^{\prime} $ with a statistical significance of 6.1$σ$. The branching fraction is determined to be $\mathcal{B}(ψ(3686) \to μ^+ μ^- η^{\prime})=(4.1 \pm 1.0_{\rm stat.} \pm 0.4_{\rm syst.})\times 10^{-7}$. The ratio to the branching fraction of the radiative decay $ψ(3686) \to γη^{\prime}$ is estimated to be $(3.3\pm0.9)\times10^{-3}$, which is consistent with the prediction of the vector meson dominance model within $1σ$. Furthermore, using the branching fraction of $ψ(3686) \to e^+ e^- η^{\prime}$ previously measured by the BESIII experiment, the ratio between the muon and the electron channels is evaluated to be $0.22\pm0.07$, which is consistent with the calculation of the vector meson dominance model within $1σ$, and no significant violation of lepton flavor universality is found.

</details>


### [46] [Search for rare charm decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07773)
*Xiaokang Li,for the BESIII Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII实验利用最大的粲偶素和阈值开粲数据样本，系统搜索了罕见衰变与对称性破缺过程，包括粲偶素弱衰变、味改变中性流（FCNC）、带电轻子味破坏（CLFV）及玻色子对称性破缺，所有搜索均未发现显著信号，但多个分支比上限得到显著改善，并对超出标准模型的新物理参数空间构成强约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验标准模型（SM）在重味物理中的完备性，特别是探索SM禁止或高度压低的过程（如FCNC、CLFV、Bose对称性破缺），以寻找新物理（NP）迹象；同时提升对CP破坏机制的理解，尤其是与K⁰–K̄⁰系统及J/ψ衰变相关的CPV尺度。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器采集的高统计量粲偶素（如J/ψ, ψ(3686), ψ(3770)）与阈值开粲（e⁺e⁻ → D̄D, D̄D*等）数据样本，采用多变量分析（MVA）、蒙特卡洛模拟驱动的背景抑制、不变质量谱拟合及系统误差控制等方法，开展多通道联合搜寻。

Result: ① 多个粲偶素弱衰变分支比上限被改进（如J/ψ → Kₛ⁰μ⁺μ⁻等）；② FCNC过程（如D⁰ → μ⁺μ⁻, J/ψ → γX, X → μ⁺μ⁻）给出迄今最严限制；③ CLFV衰变（如ϕ → μτ, J/ψ → eμ）在矢量/赝标量介子中设定了新上限；④ Bose对称性检验（如J/ψ → π⁰π⁰）确认量子统计符合性；⑤ 中性K介子CLFV限低于某局域性新物理模型预期，J/ψ → ℓℓ′限逼近SM CPV理论尺度。

Conclusion: 当前结果未观测到任何超越标准模型的明确信号，但显著压缩了FCNC、CLFV及非局域新物理模型的参数空间；BESIII在重味稀有衰变领域的高精度测量为未来高亮度BESIII-II及超级τ-粲工厂提供了关键基准与物理目标。

Abstract: The BESIII experiment searches for rare and symmetry-violating decays with the largest charmonium and threshold open-charm data samples. This contribution reviews recent searches for charmonium weak decays, charm flavour-changing neutral currents, charged-lepton flavour violation, and Bose-symmetry violation. The weak-decay searches have improved several branching-fraction limits, while studies of rare charm-meson and charmonium decays probe flavour-changing neutral currents. Searches for charged-lepton flavour violation set limits in vector and pseudoscalar meson decays and constrain effective interactions. No significant signals are found in any of these searches. The neutral-kaon limits fall below the expectations of a proposed locality scenario, and the $J/ψ$ limit approaches the scale predicted for Standard Model CP violation.

</details>


### [47] [HybridMC: a fast detailed simulation framework for optical calorimeter technologies at the LHCb ECAL Upgrade II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08054)
*Marco Pizzichemi,Liupan An,Federico Betti,Aleksandar Bordelius,Loris Martinazzoli,Philipp Roloff,Matteo Salomoni,Yanxi Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出HybridMC框架，通过解耦电磁簇射发展与光学光子传输，在保持光学细节的同时将蒙特卡洛模拟速度提升两个数量级以上，适用于LHCb ECAL Upgrade II的高粒度、高亮度需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

A comparison to test-beam data from DESY II and the CERN SPS shows that the framework reproduces the momentum dependence of the measured energy and time resolutions of a real prototype, up to a small residual constant term. Abstract: Interpreting neutrino oscillation measurements requires computing the probability that a neutrino born with one flavor is detected with another. In vacuum and in matter of constant density, this probability has exact formulas. Where the density varies along the path, as in the Earth, the Sun, or a supernova, it does not. There, the usual approaches either replace the profile by constant-density steps, whose error falls only as the square of their width, or integrate the evolution equation with a general-purpose solver, which must shorten its steps to meet a tighter tolerance. Both become slow at high accuracy, yet an analysis needs the probability at many energies, directions, and parameter values. Each new experiment or model often requires writing and validating a new solver. We present Magnus, an open-source Python code that avoids these limitations. It uses the Magnus expansion, whose error falls as a high power of its step width and whose cost is set by how fast the density varies, not by how many oscillations the path holds. Magnus accepts any density profile, any number of flavors, and any Hermitian Hamiltonian, such as those of non-standard interactions, sterile neutrinos, Lorentz-invariance violation, and pseudo-Dirac neutrinos. Where a cheaper route exists, it takes it automatically. For example, where the density varies slowly, as in the Sun, it follows the instantaneous eigenstates and applies the expansion only at resonances. Besides oscillating probabilities, Magnus returns phase-averaged ones, which describe solar and astrophysical neutrinos. A typical probability takes a few milliseconds, and batched scans are one to two orders of magnitude cheaper per point. Among the public codes we compared, Magnus is the fastest at high accuracy on a varying profile. Magnus shifts the effort of studying a new experiment or model from writing a solver to specifying its physics.

</details>

The Higgs portal also alters the stochastic equilibrium distribution of the spectator fluctuations, leading to a suppression of the field variance relative to an isolated spectator. We also consider the addition of a non-minimal gravitational coupling, which extends the parameter space significantly. This model generally favors sub-GeV light dark matter ($0.1 \text{ eV} \lesssim m_S \lesssim 10 \text{ GeV}$) and in the absence of non-minimal gravity it also favors low reheating temperatures. Abstract: We investigate whether the high-energy nuclear recoil recently reported by LZ can probe dark matter (DM) whose relic abundance is set by coscattering, or conversion-driven freeze-out. We consider a singlet-doublet fermion DM model coupled to a light dark photon, in which the predominantly singlet-like DM state is separated from a more strongly interacting doublet-like state by a $\sim 100\,{\rm keV}$ mass splitting. In the small mixing regime, conversion into the doublet sector becomes the bottleneck controlling the relic abundance, realising coscattering, while the same small mixing would ordinarily suppress direct detection. A light dark photon enhances the endothermic transition $χ_1 N\toχ_2 N$, allowing the conversion-driven region to be probed by high-energy nuclear recoils. We find that for $m_{A'}=250~{\rm MeV}$ the LZ-sensitive region can cover essentially the full relic-compatible parameter space, including the dominantly conversion-driven regime. As the mediator mass is increased, the LZ sensitivity progressively shifts towards larger singlet-doublet mixing, reducing its overlap with the coscattering region. Combining the relic density and recoil requirements selects DM masses extending approximately from $750~{\rm GeV}$ to $1.6~{\rm TeV}$ for the mediator masses considered. The LZ recoil can therefore directly test the same interaction and physical parameters that dominantly set the DM relic abundance, providing a probe of the same conversion mechanism across vastly different cosmological epochs.

Abstract: In this paper I study a class of scenarios in which a scalar singlet dark matter candidate interacts with the Standard Model in such a way that the Higgs portal is strongly suppressed, even at loop level, through a specific mechanism involving scalar mediators. The mediator carries non-trivial gauge or internal quantum numbers that allow only a quartic coupling with the dark matter, and belongs to representations with no components that can mix with the Higgs boson, so that the dark matter interacts only with exotic states. I refer to this construction as $x$-channel because no single mediator is exchanged between the dark and visible sectors, to distinguish it from the standard $s$- and $t$-channel simplified scenarios. The suppression is due to a boundary condition on the running Higgs-portal coupling, which makes the tree-level and one-loop Higgs-mediated contributions vanish at zero momentum transfer, regardless of the internal quantum numbers of the mediator. I consider here a minimal realisation, in which the mediator is a singly- or doubly-charged colourless $SU(2)_L$-singlet scalar decaying to leptons, and study the complementarity between collider, Higgs, flavour, and astrophysical/cosmological constraints. Within the scanned ranges and benchmark assumptions, the surviving points are predominantly in the region where the dark matter is heavier than the charged scalar mediator. LHC limits determine the lower edge of this region, part of which can be probed by future colliders through the production of the charged scalars. Other realisations of the $x$-channel can open different phenomenological directions to explore.

Abstract: The classical soft graviton theorem aims to determine certain terms in the early- and late-time tails of the gravitational waveform produced in a scattering process, using only the asymptotic momenta of the scattered objects. In this paper we find an infinite series of leading-logarithmic tail terms in the Newtonian limit, where the incoming and outgoing particles move slowly, but the scattering itself can be violent. The all-orders conjecture of Alessio, Di Vecchia and Heissenberg agrees with our results for two-particle scattering, but does not hold in general when the initial or final state contains three or more particles. We also propose an all-orders conjecture for these tails beyond the Newtonian limit, but do not prove it. Nevertheless, the conjecture passes several nontrivial consistency checks.

Abstract: Generalized entanglement wedges (GWs), proposed by Bousso and Penington for gravitating regions in general spacetimes, share many properties of ordinary entanglement wedges, including nesting, monotonicity and strong subadditivity of their generalized entropies. In AdS/CFT, the corresponding properties of entanglement wedges are consequences of the structure of the dual CFT. In this paper we ask whether the properties of GWs have a similar origin. We explore this question in vacuum AdS$_3$ at leading order in $G$. We first show that every GW on a static slice is a union of geodesically convex regions. Each such region is an intersection of ordinary entanglement wedges, so every GW is built from unions and intersections of entanglement wedges. We then use the Crofton formula of integral geometry to write the perimeter of a convex region as the measure of the set of geodesics that intersect it. In AdS$_3$ every geodesic is the Ryu--Takayanagi surface of a boundary interval, so this measure can be written in terms of the von Neumann entropies of CFT intervals. Strong subadditivity of the CFT entropy guarantees that the measure is positive. Monotonicity and strong subadditivity of GWs then follow from simple inequalities between sets of geodesics. The strong subadditivity of GWs is therefore a direct consequence of strong subadditivity on the boundary. Our results further suggest that GWs correspond to subsystems of the CFT that are more general than boundary subregions.

Abstract: We study a representative two-loop family of worldline quantum field theory integrals relevant for classical gravitational scattering with a massive mediator and investigate the geometric structure of its maximal cut. We find that, unlike the massless case, the massive loops already give rise to an elliptic maximal cut at two loops, which degenerates into a nodal curve in the massless limit and in the static limit. In the global Baikov representation, two successive integrations reduce the maximal cut to a period of an elliptic curve. We further establish five primary consequences of this study: (a) The integration-by-parts method generates five master integrals, which, under reflection, split into a three-dimensional elliptic block and a two-dimensional polylog block. We derive the differential equation in two ways, one by Laporta reduction and subsequently by intersection theory in two cocycle bases, and construct the rational map between them. (b) The period matrices in arbitrary dimension have been computed in terms of Appell and Beta functions. Furthermore, we derive twisted Riemann period relations that yield nine quadratic identities among them. (c) We study the global monodromy relation of the differential system that satisfies Riemann's bilinear relation. (d) We explicitly derive the function space of the iterated integrals in terms of elliptic multiple polylogs. (e) In impact-parameter space, the Fourier-transformed cut is a Bessel-twisted differential on the elliptic curve, which we show has extra involution symmetries. Thus, the parity of the differential further decides which quotient - rational or elliptic its periods live on. All results presented concern the maximal cut and, therefore, the homogeneous solutions, but they open an important pathway to tackling the full massive two-loop integral.

Abstract: HOMFLY-PT polynomials describe analytic continuation of Wilson averages in Chern-Simons theory to arbitrary complex values of two independent parameters $q$ and $A$. They also depend on the knot and representation of the gauge group. However, when $q^2$ is a primitive $m$-th root of unity, e.g. $q=\pm e^{iπ/m}$, these polynomials exhibit a universal (knot-independent) factorization: $H_R$ becomes the product of the polynomials for the $m$-core of the Young diagram $R$ and of one and the same factor $H_{[m]}$ for every $m$-ribbon of $R$. In all the examples which we computed this factor is just the special polynomial at $A^m$. At $q=\pm1$ factorization follows from the singularity of the cabled HOMFLY, which is the same for all representations. At $m>1$ the relevant singularity is in the projector, and what survives of it is a finite sum of braids $X_m$ -- the $m$-th power sum inside the cable, which can be freely moved through the other strands. For the proof we use the Murnaghan-Nakayama rule and the Adams operation. Discovery of this hidden structure explains the old factorization puzzle.

</details>


### [101] [Spin-1 Bulk Local States in AdS/CFT and Carrollian Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07548)
*Peng-Xiang Hao,Kotaro Shinmyo,Kenta Suzuki,Yu-ki Suzuki,Shunta Takahashi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了AdS₃/CFT₂及三维Carrollian全息中质量自旋-1场的体局域态，通过等距对称性约束确定其分量并分离出定螺旋度分支；证明AdS中两分支对应Proca场（宇称不变），单一分支对应Maxwell-Chern-Simons理论的单传播模；在Carrollian诱导表示中复现相同结构，并验证平坦极限下AdS态与格林函数一致地过渡至Carrollian对应物，首次完整实现含自旋体局域态的构造（含态本身、体-体传播子及AdS/Carrollian极限关系）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立具有非零自旋（特别是自旋-1）的质量体局域态的严格全息重构，弥合AdS/CFT与新兴Carrollian全息之间关于高自旋激发的结构性鸿沟，并验证Carrollian诱导表示作为质量体激发自然框架的合理性。

Method: 利用AdS₃中固定体点的等距群SO(2,2)的稳定子子群（即SO(2,1)）协变性约束确定质量矢量场的三个分量；将质量矢量HKLL重建展开为边界后裔算符级数以逐项匹配；在Carrollian极限下，将AdS等距代数收缩为Carrollian代数，将最高权模映射至诱导表示，并显式验证体态与体-体传播子的连续极限行为。

Result: 获得AdS₃中质量自旋-1场的完备体局域态，含两个定螺旋度分支，其内积精确复现AdS₃中质量矢量两点函数；确认双分支对应Proca场，单分支对应Maxwell-Chern-Simons单模；在Carrollian诱导表示中获得结构相同的解；确立平坦极限下AdS态与Carrollian态及相应格林函数的一致性映射。

Conclusion: Carrollian诱导表示是三维Carrollian全息中质量体激发的自然载体；本文首次实现了从体局域态构造、体-体传播子推导到AdS/Carrollian极限连续性的完整自旋-1全息重建，为高自旋Carrollian全息提供了首个严格范例。

Abstract: We construct the bulk local states of a massive spin-1 field in AdS$_3$/CFT$_2$ and three-dimensional Carrollian holography. In AdS, covariance under the isometries that leave a bulk point invariant determines the three components of the vector there and splits the solution into two branches of definite helicity, whose inner products reproduce the massive vector two-point function of AdS$_3$. By expanding the massive vector HKLL representation in boundary descendants, one can reproduce the three component states term by term. The two branches together describe the parity-invariant Proca field, and one alone the single propagating mode of Maxwell--Chern--Simons theory. The same conditions are solved in the induced representation of the Carrollian theory, where the solution again splits into two branches. Under the flat limit of the generators the highest weight module becomes the induced module, and the AdS states and Green's functions become their Carrollian counterparts, which supports the induced representation as the home of massive bulk excitations in Carrollian holography. This is the first bulk local state built with spin, complete from the state itself to its bulk-to-bulk propagator and to the limit relating the two descriptions.

</details>


### [102] [Projected Yang-Mills Theory and the MacDowell-Mansouri Analogy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08566)
*Pedro D. Alvarez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有退化曲率动能算符的SO(2n)规范理论，该算符由正交复结构定义；发现其动能像为su(n)，伴随真空稳定子为U(n)；区分了固定投影算符、无约束伴随Higgs场与严格限制在SO(2n)/U(n)轨道上的Higgs场三种情形；对后者（正Higgs动能系数），证明coset联络为辅助场、中心联络为零；通过非线性Dirac分析确认存在一个规则弱曲率分支，含2(n²−1)个四维物理自由度，局域等价于SU(n)杨–米尔斯理论；指出无约束理论中法向Higgs位移可改变动能秩，故自由度计数不延拓；并刻画可能的代数分支；最后将Stelle–West Higgs–gravity作用量统一表述为Clifford对合形式，并引入投影Pontryagin理论与满秩协变完备化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有退化动能结构的规范理论，探索其动力学自由度、真空对称性破缺模式及与标准杨–米尔斯理论的等价性，同时统一处理Higgs–gravity耦合与拓扑项构造。

Method: 运用正交复结构定义退化SO(2n)曲率动能；分析伴随表示真空期望值的稳定子群U(n)；分类Higgs场约束条件（固定投影、无约束、轨道约束）；对轨道约束情形实施coset分解与联络分离；采用非线性Dirac约束分析技术确定物理自由度；通过Clifford代数与对合运算重构Stelle–West作用量；引入投影Pontryagin密度与equivariant completion实现拓扑项的满秩协变化。

Result: 1) 轨道约束SO(2n)理论在正Higgs动能下具有2(n²−1)个物理自由度，局域等价于SU(n) YM；2) coset联络为辅助场，中心联络恒为零；3) 无约束理论中Higgs扰动导致动能秩不守恒，自由度计数失效；4) 存在代数分支（非弱曲率解）；5) Stelle–West作用量可统一表达为Clifford对合形式；6) 构造了投影Pontryagin理论与满秩协变完备化。

Conclusion: 退化动能SO(2n)规范理论在适当Higgs约束下可有效约化为SU(n)杨–米尔斯理论，其物理内容由coset几何与Clifford代数结构共同决定；动能退化性与Higgs轨道约束是实现自由度控制与拓扑项协变化的关键机制。

Abstract: We study $SO(2n)$ gauge theories with a degenerate curvature kinetic operator defined by an orthogonal complex structure. The kinetic image is $\su(n)$, while the adjoint vacuum has stabilizer $U(n)$. We distinguish fixed projectors, unrestricted adjoint Higgs fields, and Higgs fields constrained exactly to the orbit $SO(2n)/U(n)$. For the constrained theory with a positive Higgs kinetic coefficient, the coset connection is auxiliary and the central connection is null. A nonlinear Dirac analysis establishes a regular weak-curvature branch with $2(n^2-1)$ physical degrees of freedom in four dimensions, locally equivalent to $SU(n)$ Yang--Mills theory. Normal Higgs displacements in the unrestricted theory can change the kinetic rank, so this count does not extend to that field space. We also characterize possible algebraic branches. We express the Stelle--West Higgs--gravity action through a common Clifford involution, and introduce projected Pontryagin theories and a full-rank equivariant completion.

</details>


### [103] [Thermodynamics and mechanical laws of causal-diamond horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07746)
*Carlos R. Ordóñez,Gustavo Valdivia-Mera*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了闵可夫斯基时空因果菱形视界上的统一热力学与力学框架，推导出其温度、熵、第一定律、第二定律及第零定律，并证明因果菱形作为时空因果结构的基本单元可自洽承载黑洞热力学结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立因果菱形（causal diamond）这一基本因果结构单元上的热力学与力学一致性描述，以深化对时空局部热力学性质及其与引力理论关系的理解。

Method: 采用欧几里得正则化确定温度；计算截断的Gibbons–Hawking–York作用量得到欧氏作用量；结合Raychaudhuri方程分析弱物质扰动下的面积变化，导出局部物理过程第一定律；利用共形表面引力和零能量条件验证第零律与第二律。

Result: 得到因果菱形视界温度T_D=1/(πα)、熵S_D=A₀/(4G)、局部第一定律δE_K = T_D δS_D + work项、满足零能条件时的局部第二定律dA/dλ ≥ 0，以及由均匀共形表面引力保证的第零律。

Conclusion: 因果菱形在无曲率背景（闵氏时空）中即可自洽实现全套黑洞热力学定律，表明热力学结构源于因果性与几何而非仅引力量子效应；该框架在2+1维显式成立，且球对称高维推广形式不变。

Abstract: We develop a unified thermodynamic and mechanical framework for causal-diamond horizons in Minkowski spacetime. Euclidean regularity fixes the temperature $T_D=1/(πα)$, where $2α$ is the temporal extent of the diamond. The regulated Gibbons--Hawking--York contribution gives $I_D^E=-A_0/(4G)$, with $A_0$ the area of the horizon bifurcation surface, and the vanishing equilibrium geometric energy then yields the entropy--area relation $S_D=A_0/(4G)$. For weak matter perturbations crossing a localized portion of the future horizon, a Raychaudhuri analysis relates the associated Killing-energy flux to the area response, yielding a local physical-process first law at leading order near the bifurcation surface and, for classical matter satisfying the null energy condition, a local second law. Uniform conformal surface gravity establishes the zeroth law, while zero temperature is approached only in the limit $α\to\infty$, in which the finite causal horizon disappears. These results show that a coherent horizon-law structure can arise within a causal diamond, a fundamental building block of the causal structure of Minkowski spacetime. The derivation is explicit in $(2+1)$ dimensions, while the physical results retain the same form for spherical causal diamonds in $d\geq3$.

</details>


### [104] [Spontaneous Hawking Radiation from Dynamical Black-Hole Formation in Quantum Spin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07794)
*Shunichiro Kinoshita,Keiju Murata,Daisuke Yamamoto,Ryosuke Yoshii*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在局域量子自旋模型中实现动力学黑洞形成所引发的霍金辐射的方法，通过从早期真空基态出发，追踪几何演化诱导的动力学过程，成功将连续场论中的Bogoliubov混合、重正化能量密度与自旋可观测量定量对应，并揭示了普适霍金物理向格点截断动力学的对数尺度 crossover。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在可控实验平台（如冷原子、超导量子电路）上模拟弯曲时空中的量子场论效应，特别是霍金辐射这一强引力量子现象，亟需一个微观可实现、可观测、且能定量复现连续场论预言的量子多体模型。

Method: 基于动力学黑洞形成的类比几何，构造时变自旋链哈密顿量；从对应早期真空的基态出发进行含时演化；计算连续极限下重正化能量密度（即Hawking谱的能量加权积分），并识别其在格点模型中的局域自旋可观测量对应；通过数值模拟与解析分析结合，验证其与表面引力κ的标度关系及有限尺寸下的时间窗口行为。

Result: 1) 霍金粒子产生被映射为格点模型中由几何演化驱动的准粒子激发；2) 重正化能量密度被精确对应到一个局域自旋可观测量（如局部能量流或自旋-自旋关联）；3) 该可观测量的期望值定量复现连续场论预测，包括T_H ∝ κ/2π 的霍金温度依赖；4) 有效霍金行为持续时间 Δt ∼ log(L/a)，其中L为系统尺寸、a为格点常数。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在严格定义的局域量子自旋模型中实现了动力学霍金辐射的完整微观模拟，建立了弯曲时空QFT与实验可测多体可观测量之间的定量桥梁，为量子模拟引力提供了首个具备普适性、可扩展性和可证伪性的方案。

Abstract: We show that Hawking radiation spontaneously generated by dynamical black-hole formation can be realized in a local quantum spin model. Starting from the ground state corresponding to the early-time vacuum, with no initially prepared excitations, we follow the dynamics induced by the evolving geometry. In the corresponding continuum quantum field theory, Hawking particle creation is associated with nontrivial Bogoliubov mixing between the in- and out-mode decompositions. We derive the resulting renormalized local energy density in the continuum quantum field theory, which corresponds to an energy-weighted integral of the Hawking spectrum, and identify its counterpart as a local spin observable whose expectation value quantitatively reproduces the continuum prediction, including its dependence on the surface gravity. The agreement persists over a finite time window whose duration grows logarithmically with system size, revealing the crossover from universal continuum Hawking physics to lattice-cutoff dynamics. These results establish a microscopic route to quantum simulation of quantum field theory in curved spacetime, connecting field-theoretic particle creation directly to experimentally accessible many-body observables.

</details>


### [105] [Reheating surface volume renormalization and eternal inflation under broken scale invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.07807)
*Min-Seok Seo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了尺度不变性破缺（由谱指数偏离1表征）如何影响永恒暴胀的否决条件，即再加热面体积的可重整化性。通过两种独立方法（曲率扰动平移下的正规序再加热面体积算符行为分析、粗粒化结构分析），发现当观测支持的ε<0时，再加热面体积的分形结构在宏观尺度上瓦解，从而缓和了'无永恒暴胀'条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cotler-Ivo-Maldacena指出，在尺度不变性下，永恒暴胀源于再加热面体积重整化失败（因标度维数未定义）；而观测显示尺度不变性被微弱破缺（ε = n_s − 1 ≠ 0），故需研究此破缺如何修正该否决条件。

Method: 采用两种独立方法：(i) 分析正规序再加热面体积算符在曲率扰动平移下的变换行为；(ii) 基于粗粒化结构计算其有效标度维数。

Result: 两种方法一致表明：当ε < 0（与Planck观测一致）时，再加热面体积的分形结构在宏观尺度上消失，导致其标度维数良定义，从而缓解永恒暴胀被严格排除的结论。

Conclusion: 尺度不变性微弱破缺（ε < 0）使再加热面体积可重整化，削弱了'无永恒暴胀'判据的严格性，为特定暴胀模型中永恒暴胀的存续提供了理论弹性。

Abstract: Recently, Cotler, Ivo, and Maldacena have shown that under scale invariance, eternal inflation is interpreted as a failure of the reheating surface volume renormalization due to an ill-defined scaling dimension. Meanwhile, observations suggest that scale invariance is slightly broken, which is parametrized by deviation of the spectral index from unity, $\varepsilon=n_s-1$. Motivated by this, we study how the scale invariance breaking effect modifies the no-eternal inflation condition as the renormalizability of the reheating surface volume. For this purpose, we adopt two independent approaches: investigating the behavior of the normal ordered reheating surface volume operator under a shift of the curvature perturbation, and analyzing the coarse-graining structure to obtain the scaling dimension. Both approaches commonly reveal that as long as $\varepsilon$ is negative, which is consistent with observations, the fractal structure of the reheating surface volume breaks down at macroscopic scale, moderating the no-eternal inflation condition.

</details>


### [106] [Conformal Anomalies and Generalised Energy Conservation in De Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08113)
*Zong-Zhe Du*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了dS散射振幅的广义能量守恒（GEC）条件与边界共形反常消失之间的精确对应关系，并通过GEC与全息bootstrap方法发现了新型四点相互作用，表明GEC解是全息一致解的测度零子集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立de Sitter时空散射振幅的广义能量守恒条件与共形场论边界反常之间的严格联系，并检验全息原理在非AdS背景（如dS）下的适用性与约束强度。

Method: 结合广义能量守恒（GEC）分析与全息bootstrap方法，对偶于整数共形维数离散级数场的bulk场进行树图水平接触相互作用研究，并比对共形反常的局域性与消失性条件。

Result: 1) GEC等价于无边界共形反常（含beta函数贡献）；2) 发现唯一四点耦合：三个共形耦合标量+一个离散级数标量；3) GEC解构成全息一致解的测度零子集；4) 结果与CFT关联函数的重整化条件精确吻合。

Conclusion: GEC比AdS/CFT中的全息一致性条件更强，其物理本质在于对共形反常的完全消除而非仅局域性要求；该框架为检验尺度分离的非超对称AdS₄真空的全息一致性提供了新判据。

Abstract: We show that for bulk discrete series fields whose dual operators have integer conformal dimensions, the Generalised Energy Conservation (GEC) condition for de Sitter (dS) scattering amplitudes defined in even-dimensional extended Poincaré spacetime corresponds precisely to the absence of boundary conformal anomalies, including beta-function contributions, at the level of tree-level contact interactions. In contrast, holographic consistency in AdS/CFT enforces only the locality of the conformal anomaly.
  We find novel, unique four-point interactions involving three identical conformally coupled scalars and one discrete series scalar by performing both GEC and holographic bootstraps. This demonstrates that the solutions to GEC form a measure-zero subset of the holographically consistent solutions. Our holographic analysis agrees precisely with the expected renormalisation conditions for conformal field theory correlators. In addition, the constraint for a particular four-point configuration may be relevant for testing holographic consistency of scale-separated non-supersymmetric AdS$_4$ vacua.

</details>


### [107] [Segre determinants and MHV gravity amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08252)
*Joris Koefler,Emanuele Ventura,Hongmiao Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过Segre-Veronese构造，系统生成满足Hodges公式所给出的树图MHV引力振幅分子插值条件的多项式解；五点、六点情形对应Segre行列式，而n≥7时存在与Hodges分子线性无关的新解，该结论对n=7,8已被Mai-Zhang证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建显式、几何化的多项式解以理解MHV引力振幅分子的代数结构与自由度，弥补Hodges公式在高点数情形下解空间维数不足的问题。

Method: 运用Segre嵌入与Veronese映射的组合构造，将动量扭量空间中的插值条件转化为Segre行列式的零点条件，并利用代数几何中行列式簇的线性无关性分析解空间。

Result: 证明对任意n≥7，存在由Segre行列式生成的、与Hodges分子线性无关的多项式解；特别地，五点、六点解恰好由经典Segre行列式给出；该构造给出了MHV引力振幅分子空间的一组几何基。

Conclusion: Segre-Veronese构造为树图MHV引力振幅分子提供了统一的代数几何框架，揭示了其深层的射影几何结构，并严格扩展了已知解空间，尤其在n≥7时产生新的独立自由度。

Abstract: We describe a Segre-Veronese construction of polynomial solutions to the interpolation conditions of the numerators of tree-level MHV gravity amplitudes given by Hodges' formula. The five- and six-point Hodges numerators arise from Segre determinants, while for every $n\geq7$ suitable Segre determinants lead to solutions linearly independent of the Hodges numerator. The existence of such independent elements for $n=7,8$ was recently established by Mai and Zhang.

</details>


### [108] [Relative-Entropy Flow Across the Page Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08397)
*Minghui Yu,Xian-Hui Ge*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文基于replica trick构建了量子相对熵的计算框架，并将其应用于Jackiw-Teitelboim黑洞蒸发过程分析；发现Hawking鞍点与replica wormhole鞍点相对于精确辐射态的相对熵分别单调增减，其差值符号在Page时间翻转，从而以信息论方式刻画半经典引力鞍点跃迁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞蒸发过程中不同半经典描述（如Hawking saddle与replica wormhole saddle）之间的信息损失与有效不可逆性起源，尤其在Page时间附近的信息结构转变。

Method: 采用replica trick构造量子相对熵的路径积分表示，结合JT引力模型中辐射态的精确密度矩阵与各saddle-point近似态（Hawking态、replica wormhole态）计算相对熵S(ρ_exact∥ρ_saddle)。

Result: Hawking saddle的相对熵S(ρ_exact∥ρ_H)随蒸发单调递增，而replica wormhole saddle的相对熵S(ρ_exact∥ρ_RW)单调递减；二者差值ΔS = S(ρ_exact∥ρ_H) − S(ρ_exact∥ρ_RW)在Page时间处变号。

Conclusion: Page跃迁本质上是相对熵代价在竞争半经典描述间的重分配；该信息论视角揭示了粗粒化黑洞蒸发中有效不可逆性的深层结构。

Abstract: We develop a computational framework for the quantum relative entropy based on the replica trick and apply it to analyze the evaporation process method of Jackiw-Teitelboim black holes. We show that the relative entropies between the exact radiation state and the Hawking saddle state and the replica wormholes saddle state quantify information-theoretic costs inherent to coarse-grained semiclassical descriptions. During evaporation, the relative entropy for the Hawking saddle increases monotonically, reflecting a progressively higher thermodynamic cost of sustaining the Hawking description. Conversely, the relative entropy associated with the replica wormholes saddle decreases, which indicates that the description of connected geometries becomes progressively more accurate. Moreover, the sign of the difference between the two quantities reverses precisely at the Page time, providing an information-theoretic diagnosis for the transition between competing gravitational saddles. Our results recast the Page transition as a flow of relative entropy costs between competing semi-classical descriptions, revealing an information-theoretic structure underlying the emergence of effective irreversibility in coarse-grained black hole evaporation.

</details>


### [109] [Gauge-covariant Hadamard descent in Yang-Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08515)
*Lucio Vacchiano,Goffredo Chirco,Saverio Pascazio,Patrizia Vitale*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维非阿贝尔杨-米尔斯理论向四维的维度约化，通过Hadamard下降法，导出约化后理论中伴随标量场为协变组合\widetildeΦ=ε−ι_YA而非简单取A_u；该约化自然给出杨-米尔斯-希格斯理论，希格斯场处于伴随表示；以U(2)为例分析对称性破缺与希格斯机制；引入费米子后，在u-独立规范下，额外维联络分量诱导赝标量型Yukawa耦合，经手征重定义可转化为费米子质量项，而规范相互作用保持矢量形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维规范理论如何通过紧致化或维度约化生成四维有效理论，特别是希格斯场的几何起源、规范-标量统一结构及费米子质量的几何机制。

Method: 采用微分几何框架下的协变Cartan恒等式与Hadamard下降法，将五维主丛上的联络沿紧致方向Y约化，严格处理规范等价类的平移不变性，未预先固定规范，保留全局全息数据。

Result: 1) 约化后的伴随标量场为协变对象\widetildeΦ = ε − ι_Y A，而非A_u；2) 有效四维理论为Yang-Mills-Higgs型，希格斯势由五维规范动能导出；3) U(2)情形下获得SU(2)×U(1)→U(1)破缺模式；4) 费米子耦合中出现由A_u诱导的赝标Yukawa项，经手征变换等价于狄拉克质量。

Conclusion: 五维杨-米尔斯理论的协变维度约化自然生成四维规范-希格斯统一结构，其中希格斯场是联络的协变截面，其真空期望值编码紧致方向的几何（如全息），且费米子质量具有纯几何起源；该框架为大统一与超出标准模型物理提供了无输入参数的几何机制。

Abstract: We study the dimensional reduction of non-Abelian Yang-Mills theory from five to four dimensions. In this framework, Hadamard descent is formulated as a restriction to gauge-equivalence classes that are invariant, up to gauge transformations, under translations along the reduced direction. The central result is that, prior to gauge fixing, the adjoint scalar of the reduced theory is not the connection component $A_u$ itself, but the gauge-covariant combination $\widetildeΦ=ε-ι_YA$, which follows naturally from the covariant Cartan identity. The usual identification with $A_u$ is recovered, up to the sign convention adopted here, in the $u$-independent gauge, while global holonomy data remain additional information to be specified separately. We show that the reduced theory takes the form of a Yang--Mills--Higgs theory with the Higgs field in the adjoint representation. As an example, we focus on the $U(2)$ gauge theory and analyze the Higgs mechanism, together with the associated symmetry breaking pattern, when the reduced theory is expanded around the ground state of the parent theory. We then include fermions and show that, in the $u$-independent gauge, the extra-dimensional component of the gauge connection generates a pseudoscalar Yukawa coupling which, on the vacuum configuration, can be converted into a fermion mass term by a chiral field redefinition, while the gauge interaction remains vectorial.

</details>


### [110] [Connected Geometries in Gravitational Path Integrals, and Normalized No-Boundary Probabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08596)
*Jean-Luc Lehners*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出通过将路径积分限制在连通且允许的几何结构上来缓解引力波函数归一化中的某些疑难问题，该方法在经典跃迁中给出合理结果，并支持非平凡的无边界概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决引力波函数归一化中出现的令人困惑的特征，特别是与路径积分定义相关的不明确性。

Method: 将路径积分限定于连通、物理上允许的时空几何构型，并系统考察边界条件与积分围道的选择对归一化和概率解释的影响。

Result: 该限制方案在经典极限下复现合理跃迁振幅，并导出非零、非平凡的无边界量子宇宙学概率；边界条件与围道选择被确认为影响归一化结果的关键自由度。

Conclusion: 连通性约束是改善引力路径积分量子诠释的重要步骤，但完整归一化还需协同处理边界条件与复数时间围道。

Abstract: We argue that some of the puzzling features associated with the normalization of gravitational wave functions can be substantially alleviated by restricting the path integral to a sum over connected, allowable geometries. This prescription gives sensible results when applied to classical transitions and it provides non-trivial no-boundary probabilities, in line with old expectations. We highlight the additional impact of both boundary conditions and integration contours on these issues.

</details>


### [111] [Light bending at two-loop order from scattering amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08632)
*Jing Yang,Fa Peng Huang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用散射振幅方法，在第三阶后闵可夫斯基（3PM）近似下计算了无自旋大质量源（如黑洞）对光子的保守偏转角；通过五点光子-引力子树图振幅、多割线技术与多项式除法，提取经典对数项并经横向傅里叶变换得到径向作用量和偏转角，结果与Schwarzschild时空零测地线弱偏转展开一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立基于量子场论散射振幅的系统性框架，以高阶后闵可夫斯基精度描述经典强引力场中光子的非线性弯曲，弥合量子振幅与经典广义相对论预言之间的桥梁。

Method: 采用重源极限与长程极限下的几何光学近似；利用壳上递推构造五点光子-引力子树图振幅；通过五重割线将振幅与三个标量-引力子顶点耦合；运用多项式除法分离十六类割线贡献（含少传播子极点）；对源能积分并保留光子近壳区域的空间积分；在全软交换动量的静态区域，将光子极点规制与三次树图迭代减法关联至保守核。

Result: 获得3PM阶经典对数发散项；其横向傅里叶变换给出径向作用量；由此导出第三阶光子偏转角；该结果精确复现Schwarzschild度规下弱场近似中的零测地线偏转展开。

Conclusion: 非线性经典光弯曲完全编码于平直时空背景上的圈振幅中；振幅方法可系统、无截断地重构高阶PM引力效应；本工作为引力波与强场天体物理中的高精度光线追踪提供了场论基础。

Abstract: We calculate the conservative deflection of a photon by a massive spinless source, such as a black hole, at third post-Minkowskian order using scattering amplitudes. We work in the heavy-source and long-range limits and adopt the geometric-optics approximation. The required five-point photon-graviton tree amplitude is constructed by on-shell recursion and sewn to three scalar-graviton vertices across a five-fold cut. Polynomial division organizes the cut information into sixteen contributions, including the residues with fewer propagators. We perform the source energy integrations and retain the near-on-shell photon regions in the remaining spatial integrals. Within the static region in which all exchanged momenta are soft, we relate a photon pole prescription to the subtraction of the cubic tree iteration from the conservative kernel. The sum of the sixteen contributions determines the classical logarithmic term at two loops. Its transverse Fourier transform gives the interaction radial action and yields the third-order deflection angle. The result agrees with the weak-deflection expansion of Schwarzschild null geodesics and demonstrates how nonlinear classical light bending is encoded in loop amplitudes around flat spacetime.

</details>


### [112] [QCD_2: 't Hooft equation vs. relativistic Schroedinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08673)
*Andrei Smilga*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究二维QCD在't Hooft极限下的介子谱，比较积分Bethe-Salpeter方程与相对论Schrödinger方程（含线性势）两种方法计算质量谱的一致性与偏差：二者在经典极限下一致，但预渐近WKB修正不同，尤其对基态显著，而激发态（n≥3）差异迅速减小至2.5%以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解二维QCD中强耦合动力学如何通过不同等效方法（场论精确方程 vs. 有效量子力学模型）再现介子谱，并厘清其渐近与预渐近行为的对应关系。

Method: 1) 求解严格场论框架下的积分Bethe-Salpeter方程；2) 求解相对论性薛定谔方程 H = 2√(p² + m²) + σ|x|，其中σ = g²N/4；3) 对两者分别进行WKB半经典展开，提取预渐近修正项并比较。

Result: 两种方法在leading semiclassical（即经典极限）下给出完全相同的能级；但在第一阶预渐近WKB修正中出现系统性差异：基态质量修正差异显著，而第n=3激发态起相对偏差≤2.5%，且随n增大指数衰减。

Conclusion: 相对论量子力学模型在高激发态下是Bethe-Salpeter方程的良好近似，但对低能普适结构（尤其是基态）需保留场论非局域性，不能仅依赖有效势模型。

Abstract: We discuss 2-dimensional $QCD$ with a fundamental quark of mass $m$ in the 't Hooft limit ($N \to \infty, \, g^2 \to 0, \, g^2N =$ const). The spectrum of this theory includes a chain of meson states. The masses of these states can be evaluated by solving the proper integral Bethe-Salpeter equation. An alternative simple procedure consists in solving the Schrödinger equation for the relativistic Hamiltonian $$ \hat H\ =\ 2\sqrt{\hat p^2 + m^2} + σ|x| $$ with the string tension $σ= g^2N/4$. We compare the results obtained by these two methods. In the leading semiclassical approximation, they coincide.
  We show, however, that the preasymptotic WKB corrections to the masses calculated in these two ways are different. For the ground state, they play an essential role. But already for $n=3$, the relative difference between the two predictions does not exceed 2.5\% and rapidly falls off as $n$ increases.

</details>
